    CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
    主席報告

    Dear Shareholders,                                                                                致各位股東：

    O n b e h a l f o f t h e b o a r d o f d i r e c t o r s (t h e “B o a r d”) o f D a p h n e     本 人 謹 代 表 達 芙 妮 國 際 控 股 有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」）
    International Holdings Limited (the “Company”) together with its                                  董 事 會（「董 事 會」）連 同 其 附 屬 公 司（統 稱「本 集
    subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the “Group”), I am pleased                              團」），向 各 位 提 呈 本 公 司 截 至 二 零 二 四 年 十 二 月
    to present the annual report of the Company for the year ended 31                                 三十一日止年度之年報。
    December 2024.

    In 2024, the global economic recovery remained subdued, hindered                                  二零二四年，受到地緣政治衝突升級和貿易保護主
    by challenges such as escalating geopolitical conflicts and rising                                義抬頭等挑戰的阻礙，全球經濟復甦依然疲軟。儘管
    trade protectionism. Despite external headwinds, China’s economy                                  面臨外部逆風，在有效的宏觀調控下，中國經濟持續
    continued its moderate and orderly recovery, with gross domestic                                  穩健復甦，國內生產總值(GDP)按年同比增長5.0%。
    product (GDP) expanding 5.0% year-on-year, bolstered by effective                                 然而，消費者信心依舊謹慎，拖慢零售市場的全面
    macroeconomic regulation and control. However, persistent cautious                                復甦，市場需要額外積極措施來提振消費者信心，
    consumer sentiment has protracted the full recovery of the retail market.                         刺激內需。為應對此等挑戰，本集團堅定不移地完
    Additional stimulus measures are needed to boost consumer confidence                              善其有效的營運策略，優化品牌授權和分銷業務，
    and stimulate domestic demand. To address these challenges, the Group                             並創建更完善的供應鏈系統。同時，本集團透過活化
    remained steadfast in refining its proven strategies by optimising its                            核心品牌「達芙妮」和推進一年前推出的全新先鋒設
    brand licensing and distribution business and creating a more robust                              計品牌「達芙妮.實驗室」的發展，提升品牌競爭力。
    supply chain. Meanwhile, the Group enhanced brand competitiveness                                 透過包含原創產品設計、策略性合作、具影響力的
    b y r e v i t a l i s i n g i t s c o r e b r a n d “D A P H N E” a n d a d v a n c i n g t h e   品牌代言人和創新的營銷活動的全面策略，本集團
    development of its new pioneering design brand “DAPHNE.LAB”                                       再次實現了卓越的營運績效，銷售增長優於行業平
    which was launched a year ago. Through a comprehensive strategy                                   均水平。
    that encompassed original product design, strategic collaborations,
    influential brand ambassadors and engaging marketing initiatives, the
    Group once again delivered remarkable operational performance, with
    sales growth outpacing the industry average.

    Despite lacklustre consumer demand, the e-commerce market is                                      雖然消費需求疲弱，受惠線上市場的增長和年輕消
    charging full steam ahead, fuelled by the growth of online marketplaces                           費者的強勁消費能力推動，電商市場發展仍在全速
    and the significant spending power of young shoppers. However,                                    前進。然而，由於用戶增長停滯以及現有參與者之間
    competition has escalated due to stagnant user growth and increased                               競爭加劇，整體市場競爭也日益激烈。隨著數字時
    rivalry among existing players. As the digital era redefines consumer                             代重新定義消費者對產品質量及購物體驗的期望，
    expectations for product quality and shopping experience, emerging                                新興電商平台正逐漸超越現有傳統線上平台。在市
    e-commerce platforms are increasingly overshadowing established                                   場細分加劇的情況下，本集團有效利用自身行業領
    ones. Against the backdrop of an intensified market segmentation,                                 導地位，鞏固其在「天貓」、「唯品會」和「京東」等傳
    the Group effectively leveraged its industry leadership to bolster its                            統線上平台上的影響力，與此同時也在「抖音」和「拼
    presence on traditional online platforms like “Tmall”, “Vip.com”,                                 多多」等新興平台上站穩腳步，以滿足目標受眾的需
    and “JD.com”, while also establishing itself on emerging platforms                                求。本集團透過加強與各大平台的溝通，利用他們
    such as “Douyin” and “Pinduoduo”, catering to its target audience.                                的資源，鞏固更強大的自身品牌形象，從而實現高
    By enhancing communication with these platforms, the Group tapped                                 於預期的增長和穩佔前列排名。此外，本集團持續
    into their resources to consolidate a stronger brand presence, resulting                          在具有社交媒體功能的創新銷售渠道中取得理想進

The Group also purchases financial investments, comprising wealth           本集團亦購買金融投資，包括理財產品及大額存單。
management products and certificates of deposits, which are classified      金融投資分類為按公平價值計入損益或以攤銷成本

cost.

The purchases of financial investments are carried out by the Group for     本集團乃就庫務管理目的而購買金融投資，以最大限
treasury management purpose in order to maximise the utilisation of         度地利用剩餘現金。本集團認為購買金融投資將為
surplus cash. The Group considers that the purchases of the financial       本集團提供較商業銀行一般存款回報更佳的回報，
investments will provide the Group with better returns than the returns     且並不會影響本集團的營運資金或日常業務營運。
on deposits generally offered by commercial banks, and would not affect     因此，管理層認為購買金融投資屬公平合理，並符
the working capital or the normal business operation of the Group. As       合本集團及本公司股東的整體利益。
such, management is of the view that the purchases of the financial
investments are fair and reasonable and in the interests of the Group and
the shareholders of the Company as a whole.

               to achieve board diversity through having due regard to a         慮委任新董事時，透過考慮甄選標準中的
               number of diversity factors, including but not limited to         多項多元化因素，包括但不限於性別、年

               experience, skills, knowledge and length of service, in the       知識及服務年期，尋求達致董事會成員多
               selection criteria in considering the appointment of new          元化。本公司亦將考慮有關自身商業模式

               relating to its own business model and specific needs from        選候選人士的優勢及可為董事會帶來的貢
               time to time. The ultimate decision is based on merit and         獻而作決定。
               contribution that the selected candidates will bring to the
               Board.

     4. CORPORATE GOVERNANCE FUNCTION                                           4. 企業管治職能
          The Board is responsible for performing the corporate governance             董事會負責履行本公司企業管治職務，董事會之
          duties of the Company. Terms of reference adopted by the                     職權範圍包括董事會於企業管治職能之責任，
          Board include the duties of the Board on corporate governance                即制定及審閱本集團企業管治政策及常規；審
          functions to develop and review the Group’s policies and practices           閱及監督董事及高級管理人員之培訓及持續專
          on corporate governance, to review and monitor the training                  業發展、審閱及監督本公司關於遵守法律及監
          and continuous professional development of the directors and                 管要求的政策及常規，制定、審閱及監督適用
          the senior management, to review and monitor the Company’s                   於員工及董事的行為準則和合規手冊；及檢討
          policies and practices on compliance with legal and regulatory               本集團遵守企業管治守則之情況及本企業管治
          requirements, to develop, review and monitor the code of conduct             報告披露之資料。
          and compliance manual applicable to employees and directors, and
          to review the Group’s compliance with the CG Code and disclosure
          in this Corporate Governance Report.

5. ACCOUNTABILITY AND AUDIT                                                                        5. 問責性及審核
  5.1 Financial Reporting                                                                             5.1 財務報告
      The directors acknowledge their responsibilities for                                                董事確認其編製本集團財務報表之責任。
      preparing the financial statements of the Group. The Board,                                         董事會於財務總監及財務部門之協助下，
      supported by the financial controllers and the finance                                              負責編製本集團之財務報表。於編製該等
      department, is responsible for the preparation of the                                               財務報表時，已採納香港財務報告準則及
      financial statements of the Group. In the preparation of the                                        香港《公司條例》和上市規則的披露規定，

          5.3 Risk Management and Internal Control                                5.3 風險管理及內部監控
               The Board is responsible for risk management (including              董事會負責本集團之風險管理（包括環境、
               ESG risk) and internal control systems of the Group and for          社會及管治風險）及內部監控系統及檢討其
               reviewing its effectiveness. Such systems are designed to            效益。該系統的制定為管理而非消除未能
               manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve          實現經營目標之風險；對重大錯誤陳述或
               business objectives, and can only provide reasonable and not         損失，只能提供合理的，而不是絕對的保
               absolute assurance against material misstatement or loss.            證。

               Aimed at providing reasonable assurance against material             本公司著力提供合理保證，避免出現重大
               errors, losses or fraud, the Company has established risk            錯誤、損失或欺詐，為此已成立風險管理
               management procedures which comprise the following                   程序，包括以下流程：
               process:

               •    Risk identification: identify major and significant risks       •   風險識別：識別可能影響達成本集團
                    that could affect the achievement of goals of the Group;            目標之主要及重大風險；

               •    Risk evaluation and assessment: assess and evaluate             •   風險評估及評核：根據已識別風險預
                    the identified risk according to its likely impact and the          料構成的影響及出現的可能性作出評
                    likelihood of occurrence; and                                       估及評核；及

               •    Risk management and mitigation: develop effective               •   風險管理及緩解：建立有效的監管活
                    control activities to mitigate the risks.                           動，務求緩解風險。

               In particular, procedures have been designed for safeguarding        具體而言，已制定程序保障資產不會在未
               assets against unauthorised use or disposition, ensuring the         獲授權下使用或處置；確保適當會計記錄
               maintenance of proper accounting records for the provision           的存置，為內部使用或公開發佈提供可靠
               of reliable financial information for internal use or for            財務資料；及確保遵守適用法律、法規及
               publication, and ensuring compliance of applicable laws,             規例。
               rules and regulations.

               Risk identification and assessment are performed or updated          風險辨別及評估會每年進行或更新，記錄
               annually, and the results of risk assessment, evaluation and         各職能或營運之風險評估、評核及舒緩的
               mitigation of each function or operation are recorded and            結果，並供董事會及管理層審閱。
               communicated to the Board and management for reviews.

               An internal audit department has been established to conduct         本集團設有內部審核部門，以進行本集團
               risk management and internal audit of the Group. It performs         風險管理及內部審核。內部審核部門進行
               risk-based audits to review the effectiveness of the Group’s         基於風險的審核，審閱本集團之風險管理
               risk management and internal control systems so as to                及內部監控系統之有效性，以保證規和營
               provide assurance that key business and operational risks            運之風險已被識別和管理。內部審核部門
               are identified and managed. The work carried out by the              每年最少向審核委員會報告兩次其開展之
               internal audit department reports to the Audit Committee             工作，並提供調查結果及建議。截至二零
               with its findings and makes recommendations at least twice           二四年十二月三十一日止年度，董事會已
               a year. During the year ended 31 December 2024, the Board            就本集團之風險管理及內部監控系統之有
               has conducted a review of the effectiveness of the risk              效性進行檢討，包括財務、營運及合規監
               management and internal control systems of the Group,                控及風險管理職能。
               including financial, operational and compliance controls and
               risk management functions.

               During the year under review, the principal risks of the Group       回顧年內，本集團的主要風險被識別並分
               were identified and classified into strategic risks, operational     類為可能對本集團造成重大影響的策略風
               risks, financial risks and compliance risks that might have          險、營運風險、財務風險及合規風險。這
               significant impact on the Group. These risks included:               些風險包括：

   (i)    Strategic risk                                                       (i)    策略風險

          strategic initiatives and makes timely adjustments to the

   (ii)   Operational risk                                                     (ii)   營運風險

          heavily relies on a limited number of franchisees for a                     收入嚴重依賴有限數量的加盟商的風
          significant portion of the Group’s revenue.                                 險。

          reliable franchisees to minimise potential disruptions.                     的加盟商，以盡量減少潛在的干擾。

   (iii) Financial risk                                                        (iii) 財務風險

         unwilling or incapable of performing or repaying its                        完全履行或償還其義務，從而對本集
         obligations on time and in full, thereby result in loss to                  團造成損失的信用風險。
         the Group.

          The Group regularly evaluates the creditworthiness                          本集團定期評核客戶、供應商及金融
          of customers, suppliers and financial institutions, and                     機構的信用狀況，並根據客戶的信用
          establishes clear credit limits for each of the customers                   狀況及財務穩定性為每位客戶制定明
          based on their creditworthiness and financial stability.                    確的信用額度。本集團持續監控交易
          The Group continuously monitors the financial health                        對手的財務狀況和付款行為，以識別
          and payment behaviour of counterparties to identify                         潛在違約的早期跡象。
          early signs of potential default.

   (iv) Compliance risk                                                        (iv) 合規風險
        Compliance risk refers to the frequent changes in                           合規風險是指政府政策法規的頻繁變
        government policies and regulations which may have                          化可能對本集團的業務經營產生影響
        impact on the Group’s business operations and lead to                       並導致合規成本增加。
        increase in compliance cost.

    The Group from time to time monitors and analyses                          本集團不時監控和分析即將發生的政策變
    upcoming policy changes, regulatory updates and legislative                化、監管更新和立法發展。此外，本集團
    developments. In addition, the Group regularly assesses                    定期評估監管變化對本集團營運、成本及
    the potential impact of regulatory changes on the Group’s                  獲利能力的潛在影響，並為合規相關費用
    operations, costs and profitability, and allocates a dedicated             分配專門預算，以避免意外的財務壓力。
    budget for compliance-related expenses to avoid unexpected
    financial strain.

    control systems effective and adequate.                                    效及恰當。

      The Company has adopted a dividend policy (the “Dividend                 本 公 司 已 採 納 股 息 政 策（「股 息 政 策」）。
      Policy”). According to the Dividend Policy, in deciding                  根據股息政策，在決定是否建議派發股息
      whether to propose a dividend and in determining the                     及釐定股息金額時，董事會須考慮下列因

      (i)   the general financial condition of the Group;                      (i)   本集團的一般財務狀況；

            future expansion plans;                                                  計劃；

      (iv) the Group’s debt to equity ratios and the debt level;               (iv) 本集團的負債對權益比率及債務水平；

      (v) the retained profits and distributable reserves of the               (v) 本公司及本集團各附屬公司的保留盈
          Company and each of the subsidiaries of the Group;                       利和可供分派儲備；

      (vi) the shareholders’ and the investors’ expectation and                (vi) 股東及投資者的期望及行業的常規；
           industry’s norm;

      (vii) the general market conditions; and                                 (vii) 一般市場情況；及

      (viii) any other factors that the Board deems appropriate.               (viii) 董事會認為適當的任何其他因素。

     KEY AUDIT MATTERS                                                           關鍵審計事項
     Key audit matters are those matters that, in our professional judgement,    關鍵審計事項是根據我們的專業判斷，認為對本期

     and in forming our opinion thereon, and we do not provide a separate

     The key audit matter identified in our audit is related to impairment       我們在審計中識別的關鍵審計事項是有關於投資物
     assessment of investment properties.                                        業的減值評估。

     Impairment assessment of investment properties                              投資物業的減值評估

     Refer to Notes 2.2.6, 2.2.7, 4.2 and 14 to the consolidated financial       請參閱綜合財務報表附註2.2.6、2.2.7、4.2及14
     statements

     The Group’s investment properties were measured at cost and                 貴集團的投資物業按成本計量，其後按成本減累計
     subsequently stated at cost less accumulated depreciation and               折舊及減值列賬。截至二零二四年十二月三十一日
     impairment. During the year ended 31 December 2024, the                     止 年 度， 貴 集 團 就 投 資 物 業 確 認 減 值 虧 損 人 民 幣
     Group recognised impairment losses of RMB12,789,000 (2023:                  12,789,000元（二零二三年：人民幣6,065,000元）。外
     RMB6,065,000) for investment properties. An external valuer was             聘估值師參與了減值評估，採用市場方法釐定該等
     engaged and involved in the impairment assessments to determine             投資物業的可收回金額。估值取決於若干需要管理
     the recoverable amounts of those investment properties using market         層作出重大判斷的假設，包括預期單位市場價格。
     approach. The valuations are dependent on certain assumptions               我們關注此領域乃由於其減值金額重大，以及於確
     that require significant management judgement, including expected           定該等投資物業的可收回金額時涉及重大的管理層
     unit market price. We focused on this area due to the significance          判斷及估計。
     of the impairment amount and significant management judgement
     and estimates involved in determining recoverable amounts of those
     investment properties.

     How Our Audit Addressed the Key Audit Matters                               我們的審計如何處理關鍵審計事項

     Our procedures in relation to the impairment assessment of investment       我們於投資物業減值評估時的程序包括：
     properties included:

     •    Understanding and evaluating relevant management’s controls            •   了解及評估管理層就投資物業減值評估相關的
          and processes relating to impairment assessment of investment              管理控制及程序，考慮估計不確定因素的程度
          properties and assessing the inherent risk of material misstatement        及該等投資物業可收回金額估計所涉及的判斷
          by considering the degree of estimation uncertainty and the                來評估重大錯報的固有風險；
          judgement involved in estimation of recoverable amounts of those

          and objectivity;

          methodology used;

     •    Comparing the assumptions disclosed in the valuation report to an      •   將估值報告中披露的假設條件與經參考現有公
          estimated range with reference to publicly available information;          開資料得出的估計範圍進行比較；

          methodology and the key assumptions used in the determination of           定投資物業的可收回金額所使用的關鍵假設。
          recoverable amounts of the investment properties.

     Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the          我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存在由於

          2.2 Summary of material accounting policies                                  2.2 重大會計政策概要
               2.2.1 Consolidation                                                       2.2.1 綜合賬目
                      The consolidated financial statements including the                       綜合財務報表包括本公司及其所有附
                      financial statements of the Company and all of its                        屬公司截至十二月三十一日止之財務
                      subsidiaries are made up to 31 December.                                  報表。

                      (i)   Subsidiaries                                                        (i)   附屬公司
                            Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured                       附屬公司為所有本集團擁有控制
                            entities) over which the Group has control. The                           權之實體（包括結構性實體）。當
                            Group controls an entity when the Group is                                本集團通過控制該實體以得到或
                            exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns from                       有權得到不同的回報，及通過對
                            its involvement with the entity and has the ability                       該實體的權力影響該等回報時，
                            to affect those returns through its power over the                        本集團則對該實體擁有控制權。
                            entity.

                            Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the date                         附屬公司由其控制權轉讓予本集
                            on which control is transferred to the Group. They                        團之日起全面綜合入賬，並由該
                            are deconsolidated from the date that control                             控制權終止之日起終止綜合入賬。
                            ceases.

        2.2.7 Impairment of non-financial assets                                2.2.7 非金融資產減值

             whenever events or changes in circumstances indicate                  賬面值之事件或狀況變動時進行減值
             that the carrying amount may not be recoverable. An                   檢討。倘資產賬面值超出其可收回金
             impairment loss is recognised for the amount by which                 額，則會按該金額確認減值虧損。可
             the asset’s carrying amount exceeds its recoverable                   收回金額為資產公平價值減出售成本
             amount. The recoverable amount is the higher of an                    及使用價值（以較高者為準）。評估減
             asset’s fair value less costs of disposal and value in                值時，資產乃按可個別識別現金流量

             are grouped at the lowest level for which there are                   出現減值之非金融資產（除商譽外）於
             separately identifiable cash flows (cash-generating                   各結算日檢討是否可能撥回減值。
             units). Non-financial assets other than goodwill that
             suffered impairment are reviewed for possible reversal
             of the impairment at each balance sheet date.

               2.2.11 Current and deferred income taxes                                2.2.11 即期及遞延所得稅項
                    Income tax for the year comprises current and deferred                本年度所得稅項包括即期及遞延稅
                    tax. Income tax is recognised in the consolidated income              項。所得稅項於綜合收益表確認，除
                    statement, except to the extent that it relates to items              與於其他全面收益或直接於權益確認
                    recognised in other comprehensive income or directly                  之項目相關。在該情況下，所得稅項
                    in equity. In this case, the income tax is also recognised            分別於其他全面收益或直接於權益中
                    in other comprehensive income or directly in equity                   確認。
                    respectively.

                    (i)   Current income tax                                             (i)   即期所得稅項
                          Current income tax charge is calculated on the                       即期所得稅開支按本集團經營所
                          basis of the tax laws enacted or substantively                       在及產生應課稅收入之地方於結
                          enacted at the balance sheet date in the places                      算日已實施或大致上實施之稅法
                          where the Group operates and generates taxable                       計算。管理層定期評估就適用稅
                          income. Management periodically evaluates                            項法規有待詮釋的情況的稅項申
                          positions taken in tax returns with respect to                       報情況，並在適當情況下按預期
                          situations in which applicable tax regulation is                     將支付予稅務機構的金額為基準
                          subject to interpretation and establishes provisions                 確定撥備。
                          where appropriate on the basis of amounts
                          expected to be paid to the tax authorities.

                    (ii) Deferred income tax                                             (ii) 遞延所得稅項
                         Deferred income tax is recognised, using the                         遞延所得稅項乃就資產與負債之
                         liability method, on temporary differences arising                   稅基及其於綜合財務報表中之賬
                         between the tax bases of assets and liabilities                      面值兩者之暫時差額，以負債法
                         and their carrying amounts in the consolidated                       確認。然而，如在初步確認商譽
                         financial statements. However, deferred income                       時產生的遞延所得稅項負債，不
                         tax liabilities are not recognised if they arise from                予確認；倘遞延所得稅項乃產生
                         the initial recognition of goodwill, the deferred                    自 於（業 務 合 併 除 外）初 步 確 認
                         income tax is not accounted for if it arises from                    資產或負債之交易，而交易當時
                         initial recognition of an asset or liability in a                    並無影響會計或應課稅盈利或虧
                         transaction other than a business combination                        損，則不會列賬。遞延所得稅項
                         that at the time of the transaction affects neither                  乃以於結算日已實施或大致上實
                         accounting nor taxable profit or loss. Deferred                      施之稅率（及稅法）釐定，並預期
                         income tax is determined using tax rates (and laws)                  於變現相關遞延所得稅項資產或
                         that have been enacted or substantively enacted                      結算遞延所得稅項負債時適用。
                         at the balance sheet date and are expected to

                    lease component.                                                         處理。

             The lease payments are discounted using the interest                    租賃付款按租賃內含利率折現。本集
             rate implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily              團的租賃內含利率通常無法直接確
             determined, which is generally the case for leases in the               定，在此情況下，應採用承租人的增
             Group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used,                 量借款利率，即承租人在類似經濟環
             being the rate that the individual lessee would have                    境下獲得與使用權資產價值接近的資
             to pay to borrow the fund necessary to obtain an asset                  產，在類似期間以類似抵押條件借入
             of similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar                 資金而必須支付的利率。
             economic environment with similar terms, security and
             conditions.

             To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the                        為確定增量借款利率，本集團以個別
             Group uses recent third-party financing received by                     承租人最近收到的第三方融資為起
             the individual lessee as a starting point, adjusted to                  點，並進行調整以反映融資條件自收
             reflect changes in financing conditions since third party               到第三方融資後的變化。
             financing was received.

             Lease payments are allocated between principal and                      租賃付款額在本金和融資費用之間進
             finance cost. The finance cost is charged to profit or loss             行分攤。融資費用在租賃期內計入損
             over the lease period so as to produce a constant periodic              益，以按照固定的週期性利率對各期
             rate of interest on the remaining balance of the liability              間負債餘額計算利息。
             for each period.

             Right-of-use assets are measured at cost comprising the                 使用權資產按成本計量，包括：
             following:

             •    the amount of the initial measurement of lease                     •   租賃負債的初始計量金額；
                  liability;

             •    any lease payments made at or before the                           •   在租賃期開始日或之前支付的租
                  commencement date less any lease incentives                            賃付款額扣除收到的租賃激勵；
                  received;

             •    any initial direct costs; and                                      •   任何初始直接成本；及

             •    restoration costs.                                                 •   復原成本。

             Right-of-use assets are generally depreciated over the                  使用權資產一般在資產的使用壽命與
             shorter of the asset’s useful life and the lease term on                租賃期兩者孰短的期間內按直線法計
             a straight-line basis. If the Group is reasonably certain               提折舊。如本集團合理確定會行使購
             to exercise a purchase option, the right-of-use asset is                買權，則在標的資產的使用壽命期間
             depreciated over the underlying asset’s useful life.                    內對使用權資產計提折舊。

             Payments associated with leases of low-value assets                     與低價值資產租賃相關的付款額按直
             are recognised on a straight-line basis as an expense in                線法確認為費用，計入損益。短期租
             profit or loss. Short-term leases are leases with a lease               賃是指租賃期為十二個月或者更短的
             term of 12 months or less. Low-value assets comprise                    租賃。低價值資產包括辦公設備。
             office equipment.

                    shareholders of the Company, where appropriate.

               2.2.18 Revenue recognition                                                2.2.18 收益確認
                    (i)   Licensing fee income                                              (i)   許可權費收入
                          Licensing fee income represents revenues                                許可權費收入代表來自加盟商的
                          from franchisees. Revenue is recognised when                            收益。當本集團特許產品的控制
                          control of the Group’s authenticated products is                        權轉移到加盟商，而產品交付給
                          transferred to franchisees, being when the products                     加盟商後，收益將被確認。收益
                          are delivered to the franchisees. Revenue is                            是扣除授予加盟商的回扣之淨額
                          presented net of credits granted to franchisees. No                     列賬，並排除融資成份的存在。
                          element of financing is deemed present.

                          Rebates are awarded to franchisees based on                             授予加盟商的回扣乃根據其購買
                          volume of products purchased. It is recognised as                       產品數量。它被認為是從收益中
                          a deduction of revenue and the receipts in respect                      扣減的遞延收入，有關的收款在
                          of which are deferred and recognised as contract                        綜合資產負債表中確認為合約負
                          liabilities on the consolidated balance sheet.                          債。

                    (ii) Sale of goods                                                      (ii) 銷售貨品
                         Sales are recognised when control of the products                       銷售於貨品之控制權轉移時確
                         has transferred, being when the products are                            認，即貨品送抵客戶，且不存在
                         delivered to customers, and there is no unfulfilled                     可能影響客戶對產品接收的未履
                         obligation that could affect the customer                               行義務時予以確認。
                         acceptance of the products.

                          The goods are often sold with sales discounts.                          產品銷售通常有銷售折扣。銷售
                          Revenue from these sales is recognised based on the                     收益是根據合同約定的價格扣除
                          price specified in the contract, net of the discounts.                  折扣後確認，並排除融資成分的
                          No element of financing is deemed present.                              存在。

                    (iii) Contract liabilities                                              (iii) 合約負債
                          A contract liability is recognised when the customer                    倘客戶於本集團確認相關收益之
                          pays non-refundable consideration before the                            前支付不可退還的代價，則確認
                          Group recognises the related revenue. A contract                        合約負債。倘本集團因客戶有權
                          liability would also be recognised if the Group has                     獲得以商品形式支付的回扣而存
                          a performance obligation when the customer is                           在未履行的履約義務時，亦將確
                          entitled to rebates which will be settled by goods                      認合約負債。
                          only.

    2.3 Summary of other accounting policies                                 2.3 其他會計政策概要
        2.3.1 Offsetting financial instruments                                  2.3.1 抵銷金融工具
             Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net                倘有抵銷已確認金額的法定可執行權
             amount is reported in the balance sheet when there                     力，且有意按淨額基準結算或變現資
             is a legally enforceable right to offset the recognised                產並同時結算負債時，則金融資產與
             amount and there is an intention to settle on a net                    負債將相互抵銷，淨額於資產負債表
             basis or realise the asset and settle the liability                    內呈報。這法定可執行權力不能取決
             simultaneously. The legally enforceable right must not                 於將來發生之事件，但必須執行於日
             be contingent on future events and must be enforceable                 常業務及當本集團或交易方出現違
             in the normal course of business and in the event of                   約、無力償還或破產事件。
             default, insolvency or bankruptcy of the Group or the
             counterparty.

        2.3.2 Provisions                                                        2.3.2 撥備
             Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present                 倘本集團因過往事件而須承擔現有法
             obligation (legal or constructive) as a result of past                 定或推定責任，而履行該責任時有可
             events and it is probable that an outflow of economic                  能涉及經濟資源流失，並能可靠地衡
             resources will be required to settle the obligation; and               量涉及金額，則確認有關撥備。不會
             the amount has been reliably estimated. Provisions are                 就未來經營虧損確認撥備。
             not recognised for future operating losses.

             Where there are a number of similar obligations, the                   倘有多項類似責任，於釐定履行有關
             likelihood that an outflow of economic resources will                  責任而引致經濟資源流出之可能性
             be required in settlement is determined by considering                 時，會整體考慮該等責任類別。即使
             the class of obligations as a whole. A provision is                    同一責任類別所包含任何一個項目之
             recognised even if the likelihood of an outflow with                   資源流出之可能性極低，仍須確認撥
             respect to any one item included in the same class of                  備。
             obligations may be small.

             Provisions are measured at the present value of the                    撥備乃按履行該責任預計所需開支以
             expenditures expected to be required to settle the                     除稅前貼現率計算之現值計量，有關
             obligations using a pre-tax that reflects current market               貼現率反映當時市場對貨幣時間值及
             assessments of the time value of money and the risks                   該責任特定風險之評估。因時間流逝
             specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision              而增加之撥備確認為利息支出。
             due to passage of time is recognised as interest expense.

                               other financing costs associated with dilutive                      除所得稅影響後的利息和其
                               potential ordinary shares, and                                      他融資成本；及

                          •    the weighted average number of additional                       •   假設所有具有攤薄性的潛在
                               ordinary shares that would have been                                普通股均已轉換，尚未發行
                               outstanding assuming the conversion of all                          的額外普通股的加權平均
                               dilutive potential ordinary shares.                                 數。

    3.1 Financial risk factors                                                 3.1 財務風險因素
        The Group’s business activities expose it to a variety of                 本集團業務活動面臨多項財務風險：外匯
        financial risks: foreign exchange risk, cash flow interest rate           風險、現金流量利率風險、信貸風險及流
        risk, credit risk and liquidity risk. The Group’s overall risk            動資金風險。本集團之整體風險管理針對
        management focuses on the unpredictability of financial                   金融市場之難以預測性，並尋求減低對本
        markets and seeks to minimise potential adverse effect on                 集團財務表現構成之潛在不利影響。使用
        the Group’s financial performance. The use of financial                   金融衍生工具管理若干風險需由董事會批
        derivatives to manage certain risk exposures should be                    准。
        approved by the board of directors.

               The Group is mainly exposed to foreign exchange risk
               with respect to Hong Kong Dollars and United States
               Dollars.
